加强对流感和其他流行病威胁的了解,以帮助泰国医护人员做好准备,2007-2024 年

Enhancing knowledge on influenza and other pandemic threats for healthcare personnel readiness in Thailand, 2007–2024
作者： Narumol Sawanpanyalert, Prabda Praphasiri, Darunee Ditsungnoen, Peangpim Tantilipikara, Chutikarn Pakketati, Yuthana Samanmit, Tawee Chopitayasunondh, Martha P. Montgomery, Sonja J. Olsen
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
医护人员往往是抵御新出现疾病的第一道防线,提高医护人员对流感的了解是泰国流感大流行防范计划的一部分。
设计与对象
从2007年开始,泰国医疗服务部在曼谷和泰国各地为泰国医护人员组织了关于预防、诊断、治疗以及人类季节性流感、禽流感和大流行性流感控制的培训。
核心方法
课程内容包括对全球形势、临床特征和治疗、流感流行病学和泰国负担、流感作为肺炎病因、实验室诊断、流感对高危人群的影响以及并发症风险增加的概述;泰国季节性流感 疫苗接种的流感经济评估,以及人类流感暴发的免疫和规划。
主要结果
From August 2007 through December 2024, 114 trainings were conducted, with a total of 26,422 attendees (range 10–700 per training), including clinicians, nurses, pharmacists, laboratory technicians, and public health scientists from public and private hospitals from national, regional, provincial, and district levels in Bangkok and 41 provinces nationwide.
解释与创新
主题最初集中于季节性和大流行性流感,后来扩大范围,纳入了其他大流行性威胁,例如 MERS-CoV 和 COVID-19,从而使卫生人员能够更好地应对新出现的呼吸道威胁,包括第一波 COVID-19。
科研与公卫意义
对医护人员进行流感预防、诊断、治疗 和控制方面的教育是大流行防范的重要组成部分,这些能力建设工作有助于培养卫生人力,使其能够更好地应对新出现的呼吸道威胁。
局限与证据强度
可能需要定期进修课程或在线教程来维持当前知识,并且证据强度适中,因为该研究报告了自我报告的准备情况和信心,但没有客观措施。

Enhancing knowledge on influenza and other pandemic threats for healthcare personnel readiness in Thailand, 2007–2024

Authors: Narumol Sawanpanyalert, Prabda Praphasiri, Darunee Ditsungnoen, Peangpim Tantilipikara, Chutikarn Pakketati, Yuthana Samanmit, Tawee Chopitayasunondh, Martha P. Montgomery, Sonja J. Olsen
Original abstract (English)
Healthcare personnel are often the first line of defense against emerging diseases. Enhancing knowledge on influenza for healthcare personnel is part of Thailand’s influenza pandemic preparedness plan. Beginning in 2007, the Thailand Department of Medical Services organized trainings for Thai healthcare personnel in Bangkok and all regions of Thailand on prevention, diagnosis, treatment, and control of human seasonal, avian, and pandemic influenza. The curriculum included overviews on the global situation, clinical features and treatment, influenza epidemiology and burden in Thailand, influenza as a cause of pneumonia, laboratory diagnostics, impact of influenza in high-risk populations and increased risk for complications; influenza economic evaluation of seasonal influenza vaccination in Thailand, and immunization and planning for human influenza outbreaks. Topics initially focused on seasonal and pandemic influenza and expanded in scope to incorporate other pandemic threats, such as MERS-CoV and COVID-19. From August 2007 through December 2024, 114 trainings were conducted. A total of 26,422 attendees (range 10–700 per training) were trained, including clinicians, nurses, pharmacists, laboratory technicians, and public health scientists (professionals trained in epidemiology, disease surveillance, and health data analysis) from public and private hospitals from national, regional, provincial, and district levels in Bangkok and 41 provinces nationwide. During training sessions, participants reported that the program increased their readiness and self-reported confidence to respond to potential respiratory outbreaks. These capacity-building efforts contributed to a health workforce that was better positioned to respond to emerging respiratory threats, including the first wave of COVID-19. Educating healthcare personnel about prevention, diagnosis, treatment, and control of influenza is an important component of pandemic preparedness. Refresher courses or online tutorials may be needed periodically to maintain current knowledge.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Healthcare personnel are often the first line of defense against emerging diseases, and enhancing knowledge on influenza for healthcare personnel is part of Thailand's influenza pandemic preparedness plan.
Design and population
Beginning in 2007, the Thailand Department of Medical Services organized trainings for Thai healthcare personnel in Bangkok and all regions of Thailand on prevention, diagnosis, treatment, and control of human seasonal, avian, and pandemic influenza.
Core methods
The curriculum included overviews on the global situation, clinical features and treatment, influenza epidemiology and burden in Thailand, influenza as a cause of pneumonia, laboratory diagnostics, impact of influenza in high-risk populations and increased risk for complications; influenza economic evaluation of seasonal influenza vaccination in Thailand, and immunization and planning for human influenza outbreaks.
Main results
From August 2007 through December 2024, 114 trainings were conducted, with a total of 26,422 attendees (range 10–700 per training), including clinicians, nurses, pharmacists, laboratory technicians, and public health scientists from public and private hospitals from national, regional, provincial, and district levels in Bangkok and 41 provinces nationwide.
Interpretation and novelty
Topics initially focused on seasonal and pandemic influenza and expanded in scope to incorporate other pandemic threats, such as MERS-CoV and COVID-19, contributing to a health workforce that was better positioned to respond to emerging respiratory threats, including the first wave of COVID-19.
Scientific and public-health significance
Educating healthcare personnel about prevention, diagnosis, treatment, and control of influenza is an important component of pandemic preparedness, and these capacity-building efforts contributed to a health workforce that was better positioned to respond to emerging respiratory threats.
Limitations and evidence strength
Refresher courses or online tutorials may be needed periodically to maintain current knowledge, and the evidence strength is moderate as the study reports self-reported readiness and confidence without objective measures.

PA 核酸内切酶双金属中心的参数化揭示了临床相关 突变 的动态。

Parameterization of the PA Endonuclease Bimetallic Center Reveals the Dynamics of Clinically Relevant Mutations.
作者： Luxuan Wang, Pengfei Li, Terra Sztain
摘要中文翻译甲型流感病毒通过季节性流行病和偶尔的大流行继续给全球健康和经济带来重大负担,凸显了持续开发 抗病毒药物 的迫切需要。作为抗流感疗法的最新补充,baloxavir marboxil (BXM) 以高度保守的 PA N 末端核酸内切酶结构域 (PAN) 为靶点,阻断病毒转录起始所必需的帽抢夺过程。然而,突变 耐药性的迅速出现显着降低了 BXM 的敏感性并损害了其临床疗效。PAN 活性位点内双金属催化中心复杂的电子特性阻碍了通过计算模型了解电阻的动力学,这对精确参数化提出了挑战。因此,在本研究中,我们系统地对分子动力学 (MD) 模拟的金属参数化策略进行了基准测试,包括非键合模型、键合模型和混合模型,模拟了 apo 和药物结合状态下的野生型 PAN。可靠的参数化方案的识别使得能够对五个临床相关突变体 I38T/F/M、A36V 和 E23K 进行 MD 模拟,揭示每个突变体如何重塑构象景观以调节药物结合模式。总之,我们的结果为金属酶中复杂位点的建模提供了一条途径,并为 PAN 漏洞提供了机制基础,以指导下一代抑制剂的基于结构的优化。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
甲型流感病毒给全球带来了重大的健康负担,而 抗病毒药物 对 抗病毒药物 baloxavir marboxil (BXM) 的耐药性的迅速出现损害了其临床疗效,凸显了了解耐药动态的必要性。
设计与对象
本研究系统地对野生型 PA N 末端核酸内切酶结构域 (PAN) 在 apo 和药物结合状态下的分子动力学 (MD) 模拟的金属参数化策略进行了基准测试。
核心方法
研究人员评估了非键合、键合和混合模型的参数化,并使用已确定的可靠方案对五个临床相关突变体(I38T/F/M、A36V 和 E23K)进行 MD 模拟。
主要结果
MD 模拟揭示了每个临床相关的 突变 如何重塑构象景观以调节 PAN 活性位点的药物结合模式。
解释与创新
作者将他们的发现解释为为金属酶中的复杂位点建模提供了一条途径,并为 PAN 的脆弱性提供了机制基础,以指导下一代抑制剂的优化。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为针对 PAN 漏洞提供了机制基础,可以为针对甲型流感病毒的改进 抗病毒药物 疗法的基于结构的设计提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于计算模型,可能无法完全捕捉体内生物复杂性,但临床相关 突变 的系统基准测试和直接模拟为机制见解提供了高度可信度。

Parameterization of the PA Endonuclease Bimetallic Center Reveals the Dynamics of Clinically Relevant Mutations.

Authors: Luxuan Wang, Pengfei Li, Terra Sztain
Original abstract (English)
Influenza A virus continues to impose a major global health and economic burden through seasonal epidemics and occasional pandemics, highlighting the critical need for continued antiviral development. As the latest addition to anti-influenza therapy, baloxavir marboxil (BXM) targets the highly conserved PA N-terminal endonuclease domain (PAN), blocking the cap-snatching process essential for viral transcription initiation. However, the rapid emergence of resistance mutations significantly reduces BXM susceptibility and compromises its clinical efficacy. Understanding the dynamics underlying resistance through computational modeling has been hindered by the complex electronic properties of the bimetallic catalytic center within the PAN active site, posing a challenge for accurate parametrization. Therefore, in this study, we systematically benchmarked metal-parametrization strategies for molecular dynamics (MD) simulations, including the nonbonded, bonded, and hybrid models, simulating wild-type PAN in both apo and drug-bound states. Identification of reliable parametrization schemes enabled MD simulations of five clinically relevant mutants, I38T/F/M, A36V, and E23K, revealing how each reshapes the conformational landscape to modulate drug-binding modes. Together, our results provide a path toward modeling complex sites in metalloenzymes and a mechanistic foundation for vulnerabilities in PAN to guide structure-based optimization of next-generation inhibitors.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Influenza A virus imposes a major global health burden, and the rapid emergence of resistance mutations to the antiviral baloxavir marboxil (BXM) compromises its clinical efficacy, highlighting the need to understand resistance dynamics.
Design and population
This study systematically benchmarked metal-parametrization strategies for molecular dynamics (MD) simulations of the wild-type PA N-terminal endonuclease domain (PAN) in both apo and drug-bound states.
Core methods
The researchers evaluated nonbonded, bonded, and hybrid models for parametrization and used the identified reliable schemes to perform MD simulations of five clinically relevant mutants (I38T/F/M, A36V, and E23K).
Main results
The MD simulations revealed how each clinically relevant mutation reshapes the conformational landscape to modulate drug-binding modes in the PAN active site.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret their findings as providing a path toward modeling complex sites in metalloenzymes and a mechanistic foundation for vulnerabilities in PAN to guide the optimization of next-generation inhibitors.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results offer a mechanistic foundation for targeting PAN vulnerabilities, which can inform the structure-based design of improved antiviral therapies against influenza A virus.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on computational modeling, which may not fully capture in vivo biological complexities, but the systematic benchmarking and direct simulation of clinically relevant mutations provide high confidence in the mechanistic insights.

截至 2026 年初人类流感病毒中和抗体状况的近实时数据,为 疫苗 菌株选择提供信息

Near real-time data on the human neutralizing antibody landscape to influenza virus as of early 2026 to inform vaccine-strain selection
作者： Caroline Kikawa, John Huddleston, Sam A Turner, Andrea N. Loes, Jiaojiao Liu, Sydney Gang, Tachianna Griffiths, Elizabeth M. Drapeau, Benjamin J. Cowling, FC Ho
摘要中文翻译摘要 每年两次决定是否更新 季节性流感 疫苗 中包含的病毒株,以更好地匹配最新流行的病毒株。为了表征当前 季节性流感 A 毒株的抗原特性,以便为即将做出的有关 2026-2027 年北半球 疫苗 中包含哪些毒株的决定提供信息,我们使用 57 个 H3N2 和 34 个 H1N1 流感血凝素库进行基于高通量测序的中和测定,该库反映了 2025 年末至年初毒株的循环多样性2026 年。我们针对 2025 年末收集的 302 份人类血清对该文库进行了分析。所得数据集包含 27,409 个滴度,并提供了针对流感病毒的人类中和抗体状况的近乎实时的描述。我们发现许多人血清针对 H3N2 的 K 亚群和 H1N1 的 D.3.1.1 亚群的滴度较低;这些分支最近在各自的亚型中占据主导地位。我们的测量还揭示了 H3N2 K 亚型内不同亚型的滴度差异,其中抗原区 D 和 E 中具有附加 突变 的 K 亚型菌株的滴度特别低。我们公开所有数据和随附的可视化,以便将其用于 疫苗 菌株选择以及流感 进化 和免疫分析。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在为更新 季节性流感 疫苗 毒株的决定提供信息,以更好地匹配最新流行的病毒毒株。
设计与对象
该研究使用 57 种 H3N2 和 34 种 H1N1 流感血凝素库进行了基于高通量测序的中和测定,并针对 2025 年末收集的 302 份人类血清对该库进行了测定。
核心方法
为了表征当前 季节性流感 A 菌株的抗原特性,研究人员使用反映 2025 年底至 2026 年初菌株循环多样性的血凝素库进行了中和测定。
主要结果
所得数据集包含 27,409 个滴度,表明许多人血清针对 H3N2 的 K 亚型和 H1N1 的 D.3.1.1 亚型的滴度较低,这两个亚型最近在各自的亚型中占据主导地位。此外,还观察到 H3N2 K 亚型中不同亚型的滴度存在差异,其中 D 区和 E 区中具有附加 突变 的 K 亚型毒株的滴度特别低。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为表明 H3N2 和 H1N1 的某些分支和亚变种的 免疫应答 可能正在减弱,这可以为即将到来的季节选择 疫苗 菌株提供信息。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为2026-2027年北半球疫苗的疫苗菌株选择提供信息,并提供对流感免疫进化的见解。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于 2025 年末收集的数据,证据基于该时期的单个数据集,这可能会影响结果的普遍性。

Near real-time data on the human neutralizing antibody landscape to influenza virus as of early 2026 to inform vaccine-strain selection

Authors: Caroline Kikawa, John Huddleston, Sam A Turner, Andrea N. Loes, Jiaojiao Liu, Sydney Gang, Tachianna Griffiths, Elizabeth M. Drapeau, Benjamin J. Cowling, FC Ho
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Twice each year, a decision is made on whether to update the strains included in the seasonal influenza vaccine to better match the most recent circulating viral strains. To characterize the antigenic properties of current seasonal influenza A strains to inform the upcoming decision about which strains to include in the 2026-2027 Northern Hemisphere vaccine, here we perform high-throughput sequencing-based neutralization assays using a library of 57 H3N2 and 34 H1N1 influenza hemagglutinins reflecting the circulating diversity of strains in late 2025 to early 2026. We assay this library against 302 human sera collected in late 2025. The resulting data set encompasses 27,409 titers, and provides a near real-time portrait of the human neutralizing antibody landscape against influenza virus. We find that many human sera have lower titers against the K subclade of H3N2 and the D.3.1.1 subclade of H1N1; these subclades have recently become dominant among their respective subtypes. Our measurements also reveal variability in titers to different subvariants within the K subclade of H3N2, with titers especially low to subclade K strains with additional mutations in antigenic regions D and E. We make all our data and accompanying visualizations publicly available to enable their use in vaccine-strain selection and analyses of influenza evolution and immunity.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to inform the decision on updating the seasonal influenza vaccine strains to better match the most recent circulating viral strains.
Design and population
The study performed high-throughput sequencing-based neutralization assays using a library of 57 H3N2 and 34 H1N1 influenza hemagglutinins and assayed this library against 302 human sera collected in late 2025.
Core methods
To characterize the antigenic properties of current seasonal influenza A strains, the researchers conducted neutralization assays using a library of hemagglutinins reflecting the circulating diversity of strains in late 2025 to early 2026.
Main results
The resulting data set encompassed 27,409 titers, revealing that many human sera have lower titers against the K subclade of H3N2 and the D.3.1.1 subclade of H1N1, which have recently become dominant among their respective subtypes. Additionally, variability in titers to different subvariants within the K subclade of H3N2 was observed, with especially low titers to subclade K strains with additional mutations in antigenic regions D and E.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as indicating that the immune response to certain subclades and subvariants of H3N2 and H1N1 may be waning, which could inform vaccine-strain selection for the upcoming season.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform vaccine-strain selection for the 2026-2027 Northern Hemisphere vaccine and provide insights into the evolution of influenza immunity.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to data collected in late 2025, and the evidence is based on a single dataset from that time period, which may affect the generalizability of the results.

当流感伤了心:A 型流感引起的 Takotsubo 心肌病的 病例报告

When the Flu Breaks the Heart: A Case Report of Influenza A-Induced Takotsubo Cardiomyopathy

Authors: Immad Attique, Alex Gyftopoulos, Deya Alkhatib, Raiha Shahbaz
Original abstract (English)
We present a case of Takotsubo cardiomyopathy (TCM), commonly known as broken heart syndrome, triggered by influenza A infection. Known cardiac complications of influenza include myocardial infarction, myocarditis, and pericarditis. Only a few cases of influenza have so far been reported that led to TCM. We discuss the presentation, investigations, and management of a 67-year-old woman who presented to the emergency department (ED) with chest pain and shortness of breath two weeks after being diagnosed with influenza A. We aim to highlight a rare cardiac complication of influenza infection so that clinicians can have a high index of suspicion for this association.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to highlight a rare cardiac complication of influenza infection, specifically Takotsubo cardiomyopathy (TCM), to increase clinical awareness of this association.
Design and population
This case report describes a 67-year-old woman who presented to the emergency department with chest pain and shortness of breath two weeks after being diagnosed with influenza A.
Core methods
The study involved presenting the clinical presentation, investigations, and management of the patient with influenza A-induced TCM.
Main results
Only a few cases of influenza have been reported to lead to TCM, indicating the rarity of this complication.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret influenza A as a potential trigger for TCM, emphasizing the need for clinicians to maintain a high index of suspicion for this association.
Scientific and public-health significance
Findings can inform clinicians to consider TCM as a possible complication in influenza patients presenting with cardiac symptoms, aiding in early diagnosis and management.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to a single case report, reducing generalizability, but the detailed clinical presentation provides valuable insight into this rare complication.

人工智能在传染病领域的应用、挑战和未来展望

Artificial Intelligence in Infectious Disease Surveillance: Applications, Challenges, and Future Perspectives
作者： Muhammad Naveed Aslam, Hussna Khan
摘要中文翻译传染病 监测 是全球公共卫生系统的重要组成部分,能够检测、监测和预防疾病 暴发。传统的 监测 方法通常依赖于手动报告和实验室确认,这可能会导致延迟、数据不完整和实时分析有限。近年来,人工智能 (AI) 已成为一种变革性方法,通过实现更快、更准确和数据驱动的 监测 系统来克服这些限制。本文探讨了人工智能在传染病 监测 中的作用,重点介绍了其在早期 暴发 检测、实时监测、预测建模、基因组监测 和数字数据分析中的应用。机器学习、深度学习和自然语言处理等人工智能技术可以分析电子健康记录、社交媒体、移动数据和全球健康数据库等来源的大型且多样化的数据集。这些系统已有效用于识别 暴发 的早期信号(例如 COVID-19)、跟踪埃博拉病毒期间的疾病传播以及预测 季节性流感 趋势。尽管有这些优势,但数据隐私问题、算法偏差、数据质量问题和发展中地区基础设施限制等挑战仍然是重大障碍。总体而言,人工智能通过改善早期检测、提高准确性和实现实时洞察,在增强全球疾病 监测 方面具有巨大潜力。通过适当的道德框架和全球合作,人工智能可以在加强未来的公共卫生准备和响应系统方面发挥至关重要的作用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究探讨了传染病 监测 在全球公共卫生系统中的关键作用,并探讨了人工智能如何克服传统 监测 方法的局限性。
设计与对象
该文章是一篇综述,探讨了人工智能在传染病监测中的应用,重点关注早期暴发检测、实时监测、预测建模、基因组监测和数字数据分析。
核心方法
机器学习、深度学习和自然语言处理等人工智能技术用于分析电子健康记录、社交媒体、移动数据和全球健康数据库中的大型数据集。
主要结果
AI系统有效地识别了暴发的早期信号,例如COVID-19,跟踪了埃博拉病毒期间的疾病传播,并预测了季节性流感的趋势。
解释与创新
作者将人工智能解释为一种变革性方法,可通过改善早期检测、提高准确性和实现实时洞察来增强全球疾病 监测 的效果。
科研与公卫意义
人工智能有潜力通过改进早期检测和实时监测能力来加强未来的公共卫生准备和响应系统。
局限与证据强度
数据隐私问题、算法偏差、数据质量问题和发展中地区基础设施限制等挑战仍然是人工智能实施的重大障碍。

Artificial Intelligence in Infectious Disease Surveillance: Applications, Challenges, and Future Perspectives

Authors: Muhammad Naveed Aslam, Hussna Khan
Original abstract (English)
Infectious disease surveillance is a critical component of global public health systems, enabling the detection, monitoring, and prevention of disease outbreaks. Traditional surveillance methods often rely on manual reporting and laboratory confirmation, which can lead to delays, incomplete data, and limited real-time analysis. In recent years, artificial intelligence (AI) has emerged as a transformative approach to overcoming these limitations by enabling faster, more accurate, and data-driven surveillance systems. This article explores the role of AI in infectious disease surveillance, highlighting its applications in early outbreak detection, real-time monitoring, predictive modeling, genomic surveillance, and digital data analysis. AI technologies such as machine learning, deep learning, and natural language processing allow the analysis of large and diverse datasets from sources including electronic health records, social media, mobile data, and global health databases. These systems have been effectively used in identifying early signals of outbreaks such as COVID-19, tracking disease spread during Ebola, and predicting seasonal influenza trends. Despite these advantages, challenges such as data privacy concerns, algorithmic bias, data quality issues, and infrastructure limitations in developing regions remain significant barriers. Overall, AI holds great potential to enhance global disease surveillance by improving early detection, increasing accuracy, and enabling real-time insights. With proper ethical frameworks and global collaboration, AI can play a vital role in strengthening future public health preparedness and response systems.

Question and background
The study addresses the critical role of infectious disease surveillance in global public health systems and explores how artificial intelligence can overcome limitations of traditional surveillance methods.
Design and population
The article is a review that examines the applications of AI in infectious disease surveillance, focusing on early outbreak detection, real-time monitoring, predictive modeling, genomic surveillance, and digital data analysis.
Core methods
AI technologies including machine learning, deep learning, and natural language processing are used to analyze large datasets from electronic health records, social media, mobile data, and global health databases.
Main results
AI systems have effectively identified early signals of outbreaks such as COVID-19, tracked disease spread during Ebola, and predicted seasonal influenza trends.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret AI as a transformative approach that enhances global disease surveillance by improving early detection, increasing accuracy, and enabling real-time insights.
Scientific and public-health significance
AI has the potential to strengthen future public health preparedness and response systems through improved early detection and real-time monitoring capabilities.
Limitations and evidence strength
Challenges such as data privacy concerns, algorithmic bias, data quality issues, and infrastructure limitations in developing regions remain significant barriers to AI implementation.

成人流感相关脑病、脑炎和急性坏死性脑炎:83 例病例范围界定回顾

Influenza Associated Encephalopathy, Encephalitis, and Acute Necrotizing Encephalitis in Adults: A Scoping Review of 83 Cases
作者： Veljko Rabasovic, Milan Radovanovic, Milan Jovanović, Vladimir Glusac, Nenad Stojiljkovic, Varun Jain, Nataša Radovanović, Bojana Milekic, Charles W. Nordstrom, Igor Dumic
摘要中文翻译背景:流感相关脑病、脑炎和急性坏死性脑病(ANE)是罕见但可能危及生命的流感神经系统并发症。成人病例的特征仍然很差,因为现有文献主要限于孤立的 病例报告 和小病例系列。方法:截至 2026 年 5 月 31 日,对 MEDLINE 数据库进行了 PRISMA 指导的范围界定审查。对已发表的流感相关脑病、脑炎和 ANE 成人病例进行了识别和分析,包括人口特征、临床表现、神经影像学结果、治疗 和结果。结果:纳入 1958 年至 2026 年间报告的 83 例成人病例。平均年龄为 45.6 ± 17.8 岁,其中 57.8% 为男性。三分之二的人没有任何潜在的合并症。流感发病后平均 4.6 天出现神经系统症状,其中甲型流感占感染病例的 80.7%。发烧(91.6%)、精神状态改变(86.7%)和癫痫发作(36.1%)是最常见的表现。脑炎是主要表现(44.5%),其次是脑病(31.3%)和ANE(24.1%)。MRI 最常显示大脑半球病变(53.0%)和双侧丘脑受累(36.4%),而 69.6% 的患者报告脑电图异常。总体 死亡率 为 22.9%,在 ANE 患者中最高 (45.0%)。在幸存者中,28.9% 经历了持续的神经系统后遗症。结论:流感相关脑病、脑炎和 ANE 在成人中很少见,但与大量发病率和 死亡率 相关。这篇综述代表了迄今为止报道的最大规模的成人 队列研究,并提供了对这些罕见并发症的临床谱、神经诊断结果和结果的重要见解。本综述强调了知识方面的重大差距以及需要进行多中心协作研究以改善 诊断、治疗 以及这些流感感染严重并发症的结果。

问题与背景

截至 2026 年 5 月 31 日,对 MEDLINE 数据库进行了 PRISMA 指导的范围界定审查,分析了 1958 年至 2026 年间报告的 83 例成人病例。

纳入 83 例成人病例,平均年龄 45.6 ± 17.8 岁,57.8% 为男性,三分之二无潜在合并症;神经系统症状平均在流感发病后 4.6 天出现,其中甲型流感占感染病例的 80.7%。

作者解释说,这些并发症在成人中很少见,但与大量发病率和 死亡率 相关,这篇综述代表了迄今为止报道的最大规模的成人 队列研究,为临床谱、神经诊断结果和结果提供了重要见解。

由于范围审查设计依赖于 病例报告 和小病例系列,因此证据强度中等,限制了普遍性并引入了潜在的偏差。

Influenza Associated Encephalopathy, Encephalitis, and Acute Necrotizing Encephalitis in Adults: A Scoping Review of 83 Cases

Authors: Veljko Rabasovic, Milan Radovanovic, Milan Jovanović, Vladimir Glusac, Nenad Stojiljkovic, Varun Jain, Nataša Radovanović, Bojana Milekic, Charles W. Nordstrom, Igor Dumic
Original abstract (English)
Background: Influenza-associated encephalopathy, encephalitis, and acute necrotizing encephalopathy (ANE) are rare but potentially life-threatening neurological complications of influenza. Adult cases remain poorly characterized because the available literature is largely limited to isolated case reports and small case series. Methods: A PRISMA-guided scoping review of the MEDLINE database was conducted through 31 May 2026. Published adult cases of influenza-associated encephalopathy, encephalitis, and ANE were identified and analyzed for demographic characteristics, clinical presentation, neuroimaging findings, treatment, and outcomes. Results: Eighty-three adult cases reported between 1958 and 2026 were included. The mean age was 45.6 ± 17.8 years, and 57.8% were male. Two-thirds did not have any underlying comorbidities. Neurological symptoms developed a mean of 4.6 days after influenza onset, with influenza A accounting for 80.7% of infections. Fever (91.6%), altered mental status (86.7%), and seizures (36.1%) were the most common manifestations. Encephalitis was the predominant presentation (44.5%), followed by encephalopathy (31.3%) and ANE (24.1%). MRI most frequently demonstrated cerebral hemispheric lesions (53.0%) and bilateral thalamic involvement (36.4%), while EEG abnormalities were reported in 69.6% of patients. Overall mortality was 22.9%, highest among patients with ANE (45.0%). Among survivors, 28.9% experienced persistent neurological sequelae. Conclusions: Influenza-associated encephalopathy, encephalitis, and ANE are rare in adults but are associated with substantial morbidity and mortality. This review represents the largest adult cohort reported to date and provides important insights into the clinical spectrum, neurodiagnostic findings, and outcomes of these uncommon complications. This review highlights significant gaps in knowledge and the need for collaborative multicenter studies to improve the diagnosis, treatment, and outcome of these severe complications of influenza infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Influenza-associated encephalopathy, encephalitis, and acute necrotizing encephalopathy are rare but potentially life-threatening neurological complications of influenza, and adult cases remain poorly characterized due to limited literature.
Design and population
A PRISMA-guided scoping review of the MEDLINE database was conducted through 31 May 2026, analyzing 83 adult cases reported between 1958 and 2026.
Core methods
Published adult cases of influenza-associated encephalopathy, encephalitis, and acute necrotizing encephalopathy were identified and analyzed for demographic characteristics, clinical presentation, neuroimaging findings, treatment, and outcomes.
Main results
Eighty-three adult cases were included, with a mean age of 45.6 ± 17.8 years, 57.8% male, and two-thirds without underlying comorbidities; neurological symptoms developed a mean of 4.6 days after influenza onset, with influenza A accounting for 80.7% of infections.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that these complications are rare in adults but associated with substantial morbidity and mortality, and this review represents the largest adult cohort reported to date, providing important insights into the clinical spectrum, neurodiagnostic findings, and outcomes.
Scientific and public-health significance
This review highlights significant gaps in knowledge and the need for collaborative multicenter studies to improve the diagnosis, treatment, and outcome of these severe complications of influenza infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the scoping review design, which relies on case reports and small case series, limiting generalizability and introducing potential biases.

使用蛋白质序列表征甲型流感 HA 和 NA 亚型

Characterization of Influenza A HA and NA Subtypes Using Protein Sequences
作者： Yunfei Wang, FABIOLA VACCA, Wes Rountree, Kevin Wiehe, Max M He, Tony Moody, He, M. M.
摘要中文翻译摘要 动机 流感是一种与人类过度死亡相关的传染病。对于甲型流感病毒 (IAV),表面抗原血凝素 (HA) 和神经氨酸酶 (NA) 定义了特定的亚型。IAV 的高 突变 率可能会给临床测试和测序后分配亚型带来挑战。准确的亚型分类对于追踪可能影响人类健康的流行 IAV 毒株非常重要。结果我们分析了具有已知 HA 和 NA 亚型的大型 IAV 蛋白质序列数据集。使用逻辑回归和随机森林方法,我们确定了一小组与亚型相关的氨基酸,然后将其用于开发具有接近最佳准确度的亚型表征方法。进一步分析表明,信号序列肽变异以及HA和NA中的插入缺失解释了亚型相关氨基酸的独特组合。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
流感是一种与人类过度死亡相关的传染病,准确的亚型分类对于追踪可能影响人类健康的流行 IAV 毒株非常重要。
设计与对象
该研究分析了具有已知 HA 和 NA 亚型的大型 IAV 蛋白质序列数据集。
核心方法
研究人员利用逻辑回归和随机森林方法,确定了一小组与亚型相关的氨基酸,以开发一种具有接近最佳准确度的亚型表征方法。
主要结果
IAV 的高 突变 率使得临床测试和测序后分配亚型具有挑战性,但所开发的方法实现了接近最佳的准确性。
解释与创新
作者解释说,HA 和 NA 中的信号序列肽变异和插入缺失解释了亚型相关氨基酸的独特组合,为亚型表征提供了一种新方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为改进 监测 和追踪流行的 IAV 毒株提供信息,帮助公共卫生部门应对 季节性流感。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究依赖于计算方法和大型数据集,但摘要没有报告临床样本验证或不同菌株的普遍性等局限性。

Characterization of Influenza A HA and NA Subtypes Using Protein Sequences

Authors: Yunfei Wang, FABIOLA VACCA, Wes Rountree, Kevin Wiehe, Max M He, Tony Moody, He, M. M.
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Motivation Influenza is an infectious disease associated with excess human deaths. For influenza A virus (IAV), the surface antigens hemagglutinin (HA) and neuraminidase (NA) define the specific subtype. The high mutation rate of IAV can make clinical testing and assigning subtype after sequencing challenging. Accurate subtype classification is important for tracking circulating IAV strains that may impact human health. Results We analyzed a large IAV protein sequence dataset with known HA and NA subtypes. Using logistic regression and random forest approaches, we identified a small set of subtype-associated amino acids, which were then used to develop a subtype characterization method with near-optimal accuracy. Further analysis indicated that signal sequence peptide variation and indels in HA and NA explained the unique combination of subtype-associated amino acids.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Influenza is an infectious disease associated with excess human deaths, and accurate subtype classification is important for tracking circulating IAV strains that may impact human health.
Design and population
The study analyzed a large IAV protein sequence dataset with known HA and NA subtypes.
Core methods
Using logistic regression and random forest approaches, the researchers identified a small set of subtype-associated amino acids to develop a subtype characterization method with near-optimal accuracy.
Main results
The high mutation rate of IAV can make clinical testing and assigning subtype after sequencing challenging, but the developed method achieved near-optimal accuracy.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that signal sequence peptide variation and indels in HA and NA explained the unique combination of subtype-associated amino acids, providing a novel approach for subtype characterization.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform improved surveillance and tracking of circulating IAV strains, aiding in public health responses to seasonal influenza.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study relies on computational methods and a large dataset, but the abstract does not report limitations such as validation in clinical samples or generalizability across diverse strains.

摩洛哥卡萨布兰卡住院婴儿急性细支气管炎的病毒病因学:2025-2026 年秋冬季前瞻性系列及其预防意义

Viral Etiology of Acute Bronchiolitis in Hospitalized Infants in Casablanca, Morocco: A Prospective Autumn–Winter 2025–2026 Series and Implications for Prevention
作者： Karim Zaher, Halima Kholaiq, Jalila El Bakkouri, Naima Amenzoui, Samira Kalouch, Ahmed Rguig, Assiya El Kettani, Sayeh Ezzikouri, Ahd Ouladlahsen, Ahmed Aziz Bousfiha
摘要中文翻译急性细支气管炎是全球婴儿住院的主要原因,其中呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 历来占主导地位。摩洛哥缺乏 2025-2026 年流行季的前瞻性病毒学数据。2025 年 8 月至 2026 年 3 月,卡萨布兰卡母婴医院儿科传染病和临床免疫学科进行了一项前瞻性观察性 队列研究 研究。连续对因急性病毒性细支气管炎住院的 1-24 个月婴儿进行鼻咽采样,并对 RSV、甲型流感、乙型流感和 SARS-CoV-2 进行多重快速抗原检测。共有 131 名婴儿入组(中位年龄 4.8 个月;男女比例 0.84:1)。63 名患者中至少鉴定出一种病毒(48.1%;95% CI 39.1–57.3%)。RSV 为主:49 例单独感染甲型流感,2 例合并感染甲型流感,总共 51 例阳性(病毒阳性病例的 80.9%;队列研究 的 38.9%)。甲型流感共8例(12.7%),包括2例合并感染;乙型流感病例占另外 2 例(3.2%)。未检测到 SARS-CoV-2。疫情活动于 2026 年 1 月达到顶峰(入院 65 例),2 月(34 例)和 3 月(12 例)呈下降趋势。所有检测到的病原体均已获得许可的预防方案,支持将 nirsevimab、孕产妇 RSV 疫苗接种 和 季节性流感 疫苗接种 引入摩洛哥作为公共卫生优先事项。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
急性细支气管炎是全球婴儿住院的主要原因,其中呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 历来占主导地位。摩洛哥缺乏 2025-2026 年流行季的前瞻性病毒学数据。
设计与对象
2025 年 8 月至 2026 年 3 月,卡萨布兰卡母婴医院儿科传染病和临床免疫学科进行了一项前瞻性观察性 队列研究 研究。连续对因急性病毒性细支气管炎住院的 1-24 个月婴儿进行鼻咽采样,并对 RSV、甲型流感、乙型流感和 SARS-CoV-2 进行多重快速抗原检测。
核心方法
共有 131 名婴儿入组(中位年龄 4.8 个月;男女比例 0.84:1)。63 名患者中至少鉴定出一种病毒(48.1%;95% CI 39.1–57.3%)。
主要结果
RSV 为主:49 例单独感染甲型流感,2 例合并感染甲型流感,总共 51 例阳性(病毒阳性病例的 80.9%;队列研究 的 38.9%)。甲型流感共8例(12.7%),包括2例合并感染;乙型流感病例占另外 2 例(3.2%)。未检测到 SARS-CoV-2。
解释与创新
该研究提供了摩洛哥婴儿急性细支气管炎的最新病毒学数据,证实 RSV 是主要病原体,同时还确定甲型和乙型流感是重要致病菌。研究结果强调需要针对这一人群采取有针对性的预防措施。
科研与公卫意义
所有检测到的病原体均已获得许可的预防方案,支持将 nirsevimab、孕产妇 RSV 疫苗接种 和 季节性流感 疫苗接种 引入摩洛哥作为公共卫生优先事项。该数据可以告知本地 疫苗接种 策略和 监测 程序。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于一个中心,并使用快速抗原检测,这可能降低了对某些病毒的敏感性。尽管存在这些限制,前瞻性设计和全面测试为细支气管炎病毒的局部 流行病学 提供了适度的证据。

Viral Etiology of Acute Bronchiolitis in Hospitalized Infants in Casablanca, Morocco: A Prospective Autumn–Winter 2025–2026 Series and Implications for Prevention

Authors: Karim Zaher, Halima Kholaiq, Jalila El Bakkouri, Naima Amenzoui, Samira Kalouch, Ahmed Rguig, Assiya El Kettani, Sayeh Ezzikouri, Ahd Ouladlahsen, Ahmed Aziz Bousfiha
Original abstract (English)
Acute bronchiolitis is the leading cause of infant hospitalization worldwide, with respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) historically predominating. Prospective virological data for the 2025–2026 epidemic season in Morocco were lacking. A prospective observational cohort study was conducted at the Department of Pediatric Infectious Diseases and Clinical Immunology, Casablanca Mother-Child Hospital, from August 2025 through March 2026. Consecutive infants aged 1–24 months hospitalized with acute viral bronchiolitis underwent nasopharyngeal sampling and multiplex rapid antigen testing for RSV, influenza A, influenza B, and SARS-CoV-2. A total of 131 infants were enrolled (median age 4.8 months; male-to-female ratio 0.84:1). At least one virus was identified in 63 patients (48.1%; 95% CI 39.1–57.3%). RSV predominated: 49 sole infections and 2 co-infections with influenza A, totaling 51 positives (80.9% of virus-positive cases; 38.9% of the cohort). Influenza A totaled 8 cases (12.7%), including the 2 co-infections; influenza B accounted for 2 further cases (3.2%). SARS-CoV-2 was not detected. Epidemic activity peaked in January 2026 (65 admissions), declining through February (34) and March (12). All detected pathogens have licensed preventive options, supporting the introduction of nirsevimab, maternal RSV vaccination, and seasonal influenza vaccination as public health priorities in Morocco.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Acute bronchiolitis is the leading cause of infant hospitalization worldwide, with respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) historically predominating. Prospective virological data for the 2025–2026 epidemic season in Morocco were lacking.
Design and population
A prospective observational cohort study was conducted at the Department of Pediatric Infectious Diseases and Clinical Immunology, Casablanca Mother-Child Hospital, from August 2025 through March 2026. Consecutive infants aged 1–24 months hospitalized with acute viral bronchiolitis underwent nasopharyngeal sampling and multiplex rapid antigen testing for RSV, influenza A, influenza B, and SARS-CoV-2.
Core methods
A total of 131 infants were enrolled (median age 4.8 months; male-to-female ratio 0.84:1). At least one virus was identified in 63 patients (48.1%; 95% CI 39.1–57.3%).
Main results
RSV predominated: 49 sole infections and 2 co-infections with influenza A, totaling 51 positives (80.9% of virus-positive cases; 38.9% of the cohort). Influenza A totaled 8 cases (12.7%), including the 2 co-infections; influenza B accounted for 2 further cases (3.2%). SARS-CoV-2 was not detected.
Interpretation and novelty
The study provides updated virological data on acute bronchiolitis in Moroccan infants, confirming RSV as the predominant pathogen while also identifying influenza A and B as significant contributors. The findings highlight the need for targeted preventive measures in this population.
Scientific and public-health significance
All detected pathogens have licensed preventive options, supporting the introduction of nirsevimab, maternal RSV vaccination, and seasonal influenza vaccination as public health priorities in Morocco. This data can inform local vaccination strategies and surveillance programs.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited to a single center and used rapid antigen testing, which may have reduced sensitivity for some viruses. Despite these limitations, the prospective design and comprehensive testing provide moderate evidence for the local epidemiology of bronchiolitis viruses.

用于多种呼吸道病毒检测的多重侧流免疫分析的开发和评估

Development and Evaluation of a Multiplex Lateral Flow Immunoassay for Multiple Respiratory Virus Detection
作者： Luyan Fan, Z B Liu, G Y Li
摘要中文翻译背景:同时检测甲型流感病毒(IAV)、乙型流感病毒(IBV)和严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2(SARS-CoV-2)对于快速鉴别发热患者的诊断至关重要,特别是在呼吸道病毒共循环的情况下。然而,传统的基于金纳米粒子的侧流免疫分析受到单色读数和灵敏度不足的限制,限制了其多重检测能力。本研究旨在开发一种基于多色乳胶的免疫层析试纸条,作为快速、灵敏且可靠的分析物检测工具。方法:在本研究中,开发了一种基于多色乳胶微球的免疫层析测定法,用于快速同时检测 SARS-CoV-2、IAV 和 IBV 抗原。该测定的分析和临床性能得到了系统评估。结果:该测定可在 15 分钟内进行检测,所有三种病毒的检测限 (LOD) 均为 102 TCID50/mL。临床评估显示,SARS-CoV-2 检测的敏感性为 97.73% (215/220),特异性为 100% (200/200),总体一致率为 98.81% (415/420),Kappa 值为 0.9762 (p < 0.05),与参考方法高度一致。IAV 和 IBV 的检测一致性为 100%。未观察到与五种常见呼吸道病原体的交叉反应。结论:这种基于多色乳胶的免疫层析试纸条在分析物检测方面表现出快速、灵敏和可靠的性能,无需专门培训。它显示出在 SARS-CoV-2 和流感共同传播的环境中进行家庭自我检测和早期筛查的巨大潜力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
同时检测甲型流感病毒 (IAV)、乙型流感病毒 (IBV) 和严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒 2 (SARS-CoV-2) 对于快速鉴别发热患者的 诊断 至关重要,特别是在呼吸道病毒共循环的情况下。
设计与对象
该研究旨在开发一种基于多色乳胶的免疫层析试纸条,作为快速、灵敏且可靠的分析物检测工具。方法:在本研究中,开发了一种基于多色乳胶微球的免疫色谱法,用于快速同时检测 SARS-CoV-2、IAV 和 IBV 抗原。
核心方法
系统地评估了该测定的分析和临床性能。
主要结果
该测定可在 15 分钟内进行检测,所有三种病毒的检测限 (LOD) 均为 102 TCID50/mL。临床评估显示,SARS-CoV-2 检测的敏感性为 97.73% (215/220),特异性为 100% (200/200),总体一致率为 98.81% (415/420),Kappa 值为 0.9762 (p < 0.05),与参考方法高度一致。
解释与创新
这种基于多色乳胶的免疫层析试纸条具有快速、灵敏且可靠的分析物检测性能,无需专门培训。
科研与公卫意义
它显示出在 SARS-CoV-2 和流感共同传播的环境中进行家庭自我检测和早期筛查的巨大潜力。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据没有报告与研究设计或样本量相关的局限性。

Development and Evaluation of a Multiplex Lateral Flow Immunoassay for Multiple Respiratory Virus Detection

Authors: Luyan Fan, Z B Liu, G Y Li
Original abstract (English)
Background: Simultaneous detection of influenza A virus (IAV), influenza B virus (IBV), and severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2) is essential for rapid differential diagnosis in febrile patients, particularly in the context of co-circulating respiratory viruses. However, conventional gold nanoparticle-based lateral flow immunoassays are limited by single-color readouts and insufficient sensitivity, restricting their multiplex detection capability. This study aimed to develop a multicolor latex-based immunochromatographic strip as a rapid, sensitive, and reliable tool for analyte detection.Methods: In this study, a multicolor latex microsphere-based immunochromatographic assay was developed for the rapid and simultaneous detection of SARS-CoV-2, IAV, and IBV antigens. The analytical and clinical performance of the assay were systematically evaluated.Results: The assay enabled detection within 15 minutes, with a limit of detection (LOD) of 102 TCID50/mL for all three viruses. Clinical evaluation showed a sensitivity of 97.73% (215/220) and specificity of 100% (200/200) for SARS-CoV-2 detection, with an overall concordance rate of 98.81% (415/420) and a Kappa value of 0.9762 (p < 0.05), indicating excellent agreement with reference methods. Detection consistency for IAV and IBV was 100%. No cross-reactivity was observed with five common respiratory pathogens.Conclusions: This multicolor latex-based immunochromatographic strip exhibits rapid, sensitive, and reliable performance for analyte detection without requiring specialized training. It shows strong potential for home self-testing and early screening in settings where SARS-CoV-2 and influenza co-circulate.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Simultaneous detection of influenza A virus (IAV), influenza B virus (IBV), and severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2) is essential for rapid differential diagnosis in febrile patients, particularly in the context of co-circulating respiratory viruses.
Design and population
The study aimed to develop a multicolor latex-based immunochromatographic strip as a rapid, sensitive, and reliable tool for analyte detection. Methods: In this study, a multicolor latex microsphere-based immunochromatographic assay was developed for the rapid and simultaneous detection of SARS-CoV-2, IAV, and IBV antigens.
Core methods
The analytical and clinical performance of the assay were systematically evaluated.
Main results
The assay enabled detection within 15 minutes, with a limit of detection (LOD) of 102 TCID50/mL for all three viruses. Clinical evaluation showed a sensitivity of 97.73% (215/220) and specificity of 100% (200/200) for SARS-CoV-2 detection, with an overall concordance rate of 98.81% (415/420) and a Kappa value of 0.9762 (p < 0.05), indicating excellent agreement with reference methods.
Interpretation and novelty
This multicolor latex-based immunochromatographic strip exhibits rapid, sensitive, and reliable performance for analyte detection without requiring specialized training.
Scientific and public-health significance
It shows strong potential for home self-testing and early screening in settings where SARS-CoV-2 and influenza co-circulate.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations related to the study design or sample size.

量子经典 储存宿主 计算预测流感 H3N2 抗原距离

Quantum-Classical Reservoir Computing to Predict Influenza H3N2 Antigenic Distance
作者： Mehdi Khalaj, Lingling Jin, Steven Rayan
摘要中文翻译准确预测甲型/H3N2 流感毒株之间的抗原距离对于及时选择 疫苗 毒株至关重要,但传统的血凝抑制 (HI) 测定是劳动密集型的且通量有限。我们提出 FluQRC,一种混合​​量子经典 储存宿主 计算框架,用于基于序列的抗原距离预测。在 1963-2002 年(271 株,73,441 对)和 2003-2025 年(888 株,788,544 对)的两个数据集上,FluQRC 与历史数据集上的四个既定基线具有竞争力,并且在更大、更具挑战性的 2003-2025 数据集上优于所有基线,其 MAE = 0.369,RMSE = 0.635,R 2 = 0.900 - 与最强基线相比,MAE 降低了 20.3%,RMSE 降低了 14.3%,R 2 从 0.862 提高到 0.900。这些结果证明了 FluQRC 对于大规模抗原距离预测的可扩展性和有效性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
准确预测甲型/H3N2 流感毒株之间的抗原距离对于及时选择 疫苗 毒株至关重要,但传统的血凝抑制 (HI) 测定是劳动密集型的且通量有限。
设计与对象
我们提出 FluQRC,一种混合​​量子经典 储存宿主 计算框架,用于基于序列的抗原距离预测。
核心方法
在 1963-2002 年(271 株,73,441 对)和 2003-2025 年(888 株,788,544 对)的两个数据集上,FluQRC 与历史数据集上的四个既定基线具有竞争力,并且在更大、更具挑战性的 2003-2025 数据集上优于所有基线。
主要结果
FluQRC 在 2003-2025 年组中达到 MAE = 0.369、RMSE = 0.635 和 R 2 = 0.900,与最强基线相比,MAE 降低了 20.3%,RMSE 降低了 14.3%,R 2 从 0.862 提高到 0.900。
解释与创新
这些结果证明了 FluQRC 对于大规模抗原距离预测的可扩展性和有效性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,FluQRC 可以通过提供比传统检测更快、高通量的替代方法来提高流感 疫苗 菌株选择的效率和准确性。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅基于抽象证据,虽然结果很有希望,但需要使用额外的数据集和现实世界的应用程序进行进一步验证以确认稳健性。

Quantum-Classical Reservoir Computing to Predict Influenza H3N2 Antigenic Distance

Authors: Mehdi Khalaj, Lingling Jin, Steven Rayan
Original abstract (English)
Accurate prediction of antigenic distance between influenza A/H3N2 strains is essential for timely vaccine strain selection, yet traditional hemagglutination inhibition (HI) assays are labour-intensive and limited in throughput. We present FluQRC, a hybrid Quantum-Classical Reservoir Computing framework for sequence-based antigenic distance prediction. On two datasets spanning 1963-2002 (271 strains, 73,441 pairs) and 2003-2025 (888 strains, 788,544 pairs), FluQRC is competitive with four established baselines on the historical set and outperforms all of them on the larger, more challenging 2003-2025 set, where it attains MAE = 0.369, RMSE = 0.635, and R 2 = 0.900 - a 20.3% reduction in MAE and 14.3% reduction in RMSE over the strongest baseline, raising R 2 from 0.862 to 0.900. These results demonstrate the scalability and effectiveness of FluQRC for large-scale antigenic distance prediction.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Accurate prediction of antigenic distance between influenza A/H3N2 strains is essential for timely vaccine strain selection, yet traditional hemagglutination inhibition (HI) assays are labour-intensive and limited in throughput.
Design and population
We present FluQRC, a hybrid Quantum-Classical Reservoir Computing framework for sequence-based antigenic distance prediction.
Core methods
On two datasets spanning 1963-2002 (271 strains, 73,441 pairs) and 2003-2025 (888 strains, 788,544 pairs), FluQRC is competitive with four established baselines on the historical set and outperforms all of them on the larger, more challenging 2003-2025 set.
Main results
FluQRC attains MAE = 0.369, RMSE = 0.635, and R 2 = 0.900 on the 2003-2025 set, a 20.3% reduction in MAE and 14.3% reduction in RMSE over the strongest baseline, raising R 2 from 0.862 to 0.900.
Interpretation and novelty
These results demonstrate the scalability and effectiveness of FluQRC for large-scale antigenic distance prediction.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that FluQRC could improve the efficiency and accuracy of influenza vaccine strain selection by providing a faster, high-throughput alternative to traditional assays.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on abstract-only evidence, and while the results are promising, further validation with additional datasets and real-world applications is needed to confirm robustness.

猪流感进化和台湾的一次健康治理:从人畜共患病风险管理到综合防范。

Evolution of swine influenza surveillance and one health governance in Taiwan: from zoonotic risk management to integrated preparedness.
作者： Chih-Sheng Lin, Tzu-Min Lin, Meng-Wei Lin
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
This review examines the evolution of swine influenza surveillance and One Health governance in Taiwan, focusing on the pathogen influenza A viruses (IAVs), the host swine populations, and the population context of Taiwan with 5.5 million pigs and 23.4 million people. The central question is how swine influenza surveillance has evolved from fragmented monitoring to a coordinated One Health framework to address zoonotic risks and pandemic preparedness.
证据基础与方法
这是一篇评论文章,描述了台湾兽医 监测 战略的发展,包括从分散的、事件驱动的监测到协调的 One Health 治理框架的转变。该审查包括常规全 基因组 测序、靶向血清学 监测 和跨部门的协调数据交换,以及使用人工智能技术进行大流行防范。
共识与结论
主要结论是,在“同一个健康”框架下加强兽医 监测 可以改善病毒重配事件的检测,增强病毒传播的可追溯性,并支持集约化畜牧系统的及时风险评估。台湾的经验表明,将分子流行病学、兽医现场监测和跨部门沟通相结合,为流行病防范提供了实用价值。
争议与缺口
该审查确定了 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 和人类 H1N2v 感染所暴露的通知能力方面的差距。虽然 监测 系统的建立是为了将动物、人类和环境健康联系起来,但该审查没有具体说明任何剩余的争议或未解决的机制,而是重点关注 进化 和综合方法的好处。
科研与实践启示
这些影响凸显了在“同一个健康”框架下整合分子 流行病学、兽医现场监测、人工智能技术和跨部门沟通的实际价值。台湾的经验为其他国家克服猪流感 监测 碎片化和加强大流行防范提供了可复制的途径,强调了人类和动物卫生部门协调 监测 的重要性。

Evolution of swine influenza surveillance and one health governance in Taiwan: from zoonotic risk management to integrated preparedness.

Authors: Chih-Sheng Lin, Tzu-Min Lin, Meng-Wei Lin
Original abstract (English)
Influenza A viruses (IAVs) remain a persistent global threat because of rapid antigenic change, frequent genetic reassortment, and cross-species transmission. Swine act as key “mixing vessels,” permitting co-infection with avian and human strains and accelerating viral diversification and zoonotic risk. Effective veterinary surveillance is therefore critical for detecting viral evolution within swine populations and for providing early warning at the animal–human interface. Taiwan, home to approximately 5.5 million pigs and 23.4 million people, offers a representative case for examining how swine influenza surveillance has evolved in response to these challenges. This review describes the development of veterinary surveillance strategies from fragmented, event-driven monitoring to a coordinated One Health governance framework incorporating routine whole-genome sequencing, targeted serological surveillance, and coordinated data exchange across sectors. Key historical milestones include mass vaccination and culling programs, the 2009 pandemic response and recent zoonotic detections of H1N2v in humans (2021–2023), collectively evidencing ongoing bidirectional viral exchange. Episodes of severe human seasonal influenza activity, including H3N2-associated outbreaks, further highlighted the importance of coordinated surveillance across human and animal health sectors. By focusing on surveillance practices applied to animals, this review highlights the practical value of integrating molecular epidemiology, veterinary field monitoring, artificial intelligence (AI)-enabled technologies and cross-sector communication under a One Health framework. The Taiwanese experience demonstrates how strengthened veterinary surveillance can improve the sensitivity for detecting reassortment events, enhance traceability of viral spread, and support timely risk assessment in intensive livestock systems. These lessons offer a practical, replicable pathway for other countries aiming to overcome swine influenza surveillance fragmentation and strengthen pandemic preparedness under a One Health framework. The review traces Taiwan’s surveillance evolution from fragmented monitoring of swine influenza A viruses to a coordinated One Health governance model. A(H1N1)pdm09 and human H1N2v infections exposed gaps in notification capacity. The surveillance systems, genome sequencing and AI-enabled analytics are established to link animal, human, and environmental health for pandemic preparedness.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review examines the evolution of swine influenza surveillance and One Health governance in Taiwan, focusing on the pathogen influenza A viruses (IAVs), the host swine populations, and the population context of Taiwan with 5.5 million pigs and 23.4 million people. The central question is how swine influenza surveillance has evolved from fragmented monitoring to a coordinated One Health framework to address zoonotic risks and pandemic preparedness.
Evidence base and method
This is a review article that describes the development of veterinary surveillance strategies in Taiwan, including the transition from fragmented, event-driven monitoring to a coordinated One Health governance framework. The review incorporates routine whole-genome sequencing, targeted serological surveillance, and coordinated data exchange across sectors, as well as the use of AI-enabled technologies for pandemic preparedness.
Consensus and conclusions
The key conclusion is that strengthened veterinary surveillance under a One Health framework can improve the detection of viral reassortment events, enhance traceability of viral spread, and support timely risk assessment in intensive livestock systems. The Taiwanese experience demonstrates that integrating molecular epidemiology, veterinary field monitoring, and cross-sector communication provides practical value for pandemic preparedness.
Controversies and gaps
The review identifies gaps in notification capacity exposed by A(H1N1)pdm09 and human H1N2v infections. While the surveillance systems have been established to link animal, human, and environmental health, the review does not specify any remaining controversies or unresolved mechanisms, focusing instead on the evolution and benefits of the integrated approach.
Research and practice implications
The implications highlight the practical value of integrating molecular epidemiology, veterinary field monitoring, AI-enabled technologies, and cross-sector communication under a One Health framework. The Taiwanese experience offers a replicable pathway for other countries to overcome swine influenza surveillance fragmentation and strengthen pandemic preparedness, emphasizing the importance of coordinated surveillance across human and animal health sectors.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

根据北半球 COVID-19 大流行前后测试阴性设计研究对 季节性流感 疫苗 有效性进行系统荟萃分析比较评估

A systematic meta-analytic comparative evaluation of seasonal influenza vaccine effectiveness from test-negative design studies in the Northern Hemisphere pre/post COVID-19 pandemic
作者： George N. Okoli, Sheena G. Sullivan, Diane M. Harper, Tim K. Tsang, Benjamin J. Cowling
摘要中文翻译背景:在 COVID-19 大流行期间,流感传播大幅减少。与大流行前相比,由于未接种疫苗的个体免疫力下降,因此人群免疫力的降低可能导致大流行后流感 疫苗 有效性 (VE) 的增加。方法:我们系统地回顾了已发表的同行评审的 季节性流感 VE 对抗北半球经实验室确诊流感的同行评审试验阴性设计 (TND) 研究。我们比较了 COVID-19 大流行(定义为 2020/21-2021/22)之前(2010/11-2019/20)和之后(2022/23-2024/25)的汇总 VE 估计值。使用逆方差随机效应荟萃分析计算三个年龄组(6 个月至 17 岁、18-64 岁、≥65 岁)针对 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09、甲型H3N2流感病毒、乙型流感和 B/Victoria 的汇总 VE。使用 I2 统计量对异质性进行量化,并使用 χ2 统计量测试大流行前后的汇总 VE 估计值之间的差异。结果:纳入了 85 篇出版物。6 个月 17 岁儿童(53% [42-64%] vs 37% [30-45%])和 18-64 岁儿童(35% [25-45%] vs 21% [13-29%])中,针对 甲型H3N2流感病毒 的大流行后 VE 显着高于大流行前 VE。同样,两个年龄组的乙型流感 VE 在大流行后均较高(分别为 85% [78-93%] vs 52% [45-60%] 和 73% [59-87%] vs 49% [41-57%])。相反,大流行后 18-64 岁人群的 VE 相对于 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 较低(37% [27-46%] vs 55% [47-62%])。对于 65 岁以上的人群,没有观察到显着差异,并且 B/维多利亚的数据有限。结论:证据表明,在 COVID-19 大流行之后,针对 季节性流感 的 VE 略高,尽管除群体免疫力之外可能还有其他因素可以解释我们的观察结果。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述评估了 季节性流感 疫苗 在北半球针对 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09、甲型H3N2流感病毒、乙型流感和 B/维多利亚的有效性 (VE),比较了三个年龄组(6 个月至 17 个月)的 COVID-19 大流行之前(2010/11-2019/20)和之后(2022/23-2024/25)岁,18-64岁,≥65岁)。
证据基础与方法
对 85 项已发表的测试阴性设计 (TND) 研究进行了系统荟萃分析。使用逆方差随机效应荟萃分析计算汇总 VE 估计值,异质性通过 I2 量化,统计差异通过 χ2 检验。
共识与结论
与大流行前相比,大流行后 6 个月 17 岁和 18-64 岁儿童中针对 甲型H3N2流感病毒 和乙型流感的 VE 显着升高。相反,大流行后 18-64 岁人群中 VE 相对于 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 的水平较低。≥65 岁的人群没有观察到显着差异,并且 B/维多利亚数据有限。
争议与缺口
作者指出,群体免疫以外的因素可能可以解释观察到的 VE 变化。B/Victoria 的数据有限,并且在 ≥65 岁组中没有发现显着差异,这在了解特定年龄的 VE 动态方面留下了空白。
科研与实践启示
需要进一步研究来探索影响大流行后 VE 的人群免疫力以外的因素,特别是在老年人和 B/维多利亚州。监测 应继续监测跨年龄组和病毒亚型的 VE 趋势。

A systematic meta-analytic comparative evaluation of seasonal influenza vaccine effectiveness from test-negative design studies in the Northern Hemisphere pre/post COVID-19 pandemic

Authors: George N. Okoli, Sheena G. Sullivan, Diane M. Harper, Tim K. Tsang, Benjamin J. Cowling
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Influenza circulation was substantially reduced during the COVID-19 pandemic. The consequent reduction in population immunity may have resulted in increased influenza vaccine effectiveness (VE) after the pandemic via reduced immunity in unvaccinated individuals compared to the pre-pandemic period. METHODS: We systematically reviewed published peer-reviewed test-negative design (TND) studies of seasonal influenza VE against medically-attended, laboratory-confirmed influenza in the Northern Hemisphere. We compared pooled VE estimates before (2010/11-2019/20) and after (2022/23-2024/25) the COVID-19 pandemic (defined as 2020/21-2021/22). Pooled VE against A(H1N1)pdm09, A(H3N2), influenza B, and B/Victoria was calculated for three age groups (6 months-17 years, 18-64 years, ≥65 years) using inverse-variance random-effects meta-analysis. Heterogeneity was quantified with the I2 statistic, and differences between pooled VE estimates pre- and post-pandemic periods were tested with the χ2 statistic. RESULTS: Eighty-five publications were included. Post-pandemic VE against A(H3N2) was significantly higher than pre-pandemic VE in 6-month-17-year-olds (53% [42-64%] vs 37% [30-45%]) and 18-64-year-olds (35% [25-45%] vs 21% [13-29%]). Similarly, VE against influenza B was higher post-pandemic in both age groups (85% [78-93%] vs 52% [45-60%] and 73% [59-87%] vs 49% [41-57%], respectively). Conversely, VE against A(H1N1)pdm09 was lower post-pandemic in 18-64-year-olds (37% [27-46%] vs 55% [47-62%]). No significant differences were observed for ≥65-year-olds, and data for B/Victoria were limited. CONCLUSIONS: The evidence suggests slightly higher VE against seasonal influenza after the COVID-19 pandemic although there may be factors other than population immunity that may explain our observations.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review evaluates seasonal influenza vaccine effectiveness (VE) against A(H1N1)pdm09, A(H3N2), influenza B, and B/Victoria in the Northern Hemisphere, comparing pre- (2010/11-2019/20) and post- (2022/23-2024/25) COVID-19 pandemic periods across three age groups (6 months-17 years, 18-64 years, ≥65 years).
Evidence base and method
A systematic meta-analysis of 85 published test-negative design (TND) studies was conducted. Pooled VE estimates were calculated using inverse-variance random-effects meta-analysis, with heterogeneity quantified by I2 and statistical differences tested by χ2.
Consensus and conclusions
Post-pandemic VE against A(H3N2) and influenza B was significantly higher in 6-month-17-year-olds and 18-64-year-olds compared to pre-pandemic. Conversely, VE against A(H1N1)pdm09 was lower post-pandemic in 18-64-year-olds. No significant differences were observed for ≥65-year-olds, and B/Victoria data were limited.
Controversies and gaps
The authors note that factors other than population immunity may explain the observed VE changes. Data for B/Victoria were limited, and no significant differences were found in the ≥65-year-old group, leaving gaps in understanding age-specific VE dynamics.
Research and practice implications
Further research is needed to explore factors beyond population immunity influencing post-pandemic VE, particularly in older adults and for B/Victoria. Surveillance should continue to monitor VE trends across age groups and virus subtypes.

从神经氨酸酶抑制剂到新机制:扩大流感治疗策略

From neuraminidase inhibitors to novel mechanisms: expanding therapeutic strategies against influenza
作者： Yan Sun, Puongtip Kunanusorn, Kuang Ming Tup, Santosh Chokkakula, Bing Yang
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
季节性流感 病毒(甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09、甲型H3N2流感病毒、B/Victoria 和历史 B/Yamagata)每年造成约 10 亿感染和多达 650,000 例呼吸道死亡。核心问题是如何扩展治疗策略以超越当前的限制。
证据基础与方法
本综述严格评估了 抗病毒药物 的前景,重点关注下一代帽依赖性核酸内切酶抑制剂 (CENI)、所有已批准药物类别的耐药性影响、宿主 导向疗法和生物制剂的转化障碍,以及 H5N1 大流行的防范。
共识与结论
支架级优化(例如 Suraxavir marboxil、ZX-7101A、onradivir)可以降低耐药性出现率。然而,所有批准的类别都有一个失败模式:单靶点依赖性、低适应成本耐药性 突变 以及大多数患者无法达到的治疗窗口。联合治疗显示出预防耐药性的希望,但病毒学益处并不能始终转化为住院患者的临床结果。
争议与缺口
宿主定向疗法和生物制剂面临转化障碍:它们的生物活性窗口先于临床表现,并且全身生物制剂无法在呼吸道粘膜表面达到抑制浓度。对于 H5N1,准备工作依赖于单一的观察支持的药物类别;大多数库存中不存在巴洛沙韦,并且没有针对人类 H5N1 感染的任何 抗病毒药物 的随机试验数据。
科研与实践启示
适应性试验基础设施、包括下一代 CENI 在内的多元化库存以及公平的全球 抗病毒药物 获取是流感治疗中最紧迫的优先事项。

From neuraminidase inhibitors to novel mechanisms: expanding therapeutic strategies against influenza

Authors: Yan Sun, Puongtip Kunanusorn, Kuang Ming Tup, Santosh Chokkakula, Bing Yang
Original abstract (English)
Seasonal influenza causes approximately one billion infections and up to 650,000 respiratory deaths annually. Despite decades of antiviral development, the therapeutic arsenal remains constrained by time-dependent efficacy (clinical benefit confined largely to treatment initiated within 48 hours of symptom onset), low genetic resistance barriers (resistance arising from a single point mutation), and critical evidence gaps in high-risk populations. This review critically evaluates the antiviral landscape, focusing on next-generation cap-dependent endonuclease inhibitors (CENIs), resistance implications across all approved drug classes, translational barriers in host-directed therapies and biologics, and H5N1 pandemic preparedness. Suraxavir marboxil, ZX-7101A, and the first approved polymerase basic protein 2 (PB2) inhibitor, onradivir, confirm that scaffold-level optimization (incremental structural refinement within an existing drug class) can meaningfully reduce resistance emergence rates. Across all approved classes, a shared failure pattern is evident: single-target dependence, low-fitness-cost resistance mutations (mutations that confer drug resistance without measurably impairing viral replication or transmission), and a therapeutic window that most patients do not reach. Combination antiviral therapy shows promise for resistance prevention, though the FLAGSTONE Phase 3 trial demonstrated that virological benefit does not consistently translate to clinical outcomes in hospitalized patients. Host-directed therapies and biologics face a shared translational obstacle: the biological window of maximal activity consistently precedes the clinical window of patient presentation, and systemic biologics cannot achieve inhibitory concentrations at the respiratory mucosal surface. For H5N1, preparedness relies on a single observationally supported drug class; baloxavir is absent from most national stockpiles, and no randomized trial data exists for any antiviral in human H5N1 infection. Adaptive trial infrastructure, diversified stockpiling to include next-generation CENIs, and equitable global antiviral access are the most urgent priorities in influenza therapeutics.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Seasonal influenza viruses (A(H1N1)pdm09, A(H3N2), B/Victoria, and historical B/Yamagata) cause approximately one billion infections and up to 650,000 respiratory deaths annually. The central question is how to expand therapeutic strategies beyond current limitations.
Evidence base and method
This review critically evaluates the antiviral landscape, focusing on next-generation cap-dependent endonuclease inhibitors (CENIs), resistance implications across all approved drug classes, translational barriers in host-directed therapies and biologics, and H5N1 pandemic preparedness.
Consensus and conclusions
Scaffold-level optimization (e.g., Suraxavir marboxil, ZX-7101A, onradivir) can reduce resistance emergence rates. However, all approved classes share a failure pattern: single-target dependence, low-fitness-cost resistance mutations, and a therapeutic window most patients do not reach. Combination therapy shows promise for resistance prevention, but virological benefit does not consistently translate to clinical outcomes in hospitalized patients.
Controversies and gaps
Host-directed therapies and biologics face translational obstacles: their biological window of activity precedes clinical presentation, and systemic biologics cannot achieve inhibitory concentrations at the respiratory mucosal surface. For H5N1, preparedness relies on a single observationally supported drug class; baloxavir is absent from most stockpiles, and no randomized trial data exists for any antiviral in human H5N1 infection.
Research and practice implications
Adaptive trial infrastructure, diversified stockpiling to include next-generation CENIs, and equitable global antiviral access are the most urgent priorities in influenza therapeutics.

进化限制限制了雪貂对牛源 H5N1 流感病毒的额外哺乳动物适应性

Evolutionary constraints limit additional mammalian adaptation of bovine-derived H5N1 influenza viruses in ferrets
作者： Heather Machkovech, Wanting Wei, Chunyang Gu, Lizheng Guan, Asim Biswas, Tadashi Maemura, Tong Wang, Lavanya Babujee, Peter Halfmann, Amie J. Eisfeld

Evolutionary constraints limit additional mammalian adaptation of bovine-derived H5N1 influenza viruses in ferrets

🚨 突发动态与新闻 / Health News

Published: 2026-07-23  |  Publisher: ReliefWeb
A 高优先级公共卫生新闻

全球呼吸道病毒活动:每周更新 N° 587 - ReliefWeb

Global Respiratory Virus Activity: Weekly Update N° 587 - ReliefWeb
新闻简报世界卫生组织 2026 年第 28 周的每周更新表明,全球呼吸道病毒活动较低,流感阳性率低于 10%,SARS-CoV-2 活动仍然较低。乙型流感病毒在检测到的流感病毒中占主导地位。在特定区域观察到阳性率升高(>10%):南美洲、非洲、亚洲、中美洲和加勒比地区的部分地区有流感;中美洲、加勒比地区以及南美洲和亚洲部分地区的 SARS-CoV-2;RSV 分布于中美洲、加勒比地区、南美洲、东非和南亚。传染性评估普遍较低或低于季节性阈值。该报告提供了全球概览,但没有详细说明具体的公共卫生应对措施。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布于 2026 年 7 月 23 日,涵盖 2026 年第 28 周。
地点与对象
全球,具体活动报告区域包括南半球温带和亚热带地区、北半球温带和亚热带地区、热带地区、中美洲和加勒比地区、南美洲、非洲和亚洲。
事件
世卫组织报告了 2026 年第 28 周的全球呼吸道病毒活动,其中流感阳性率低于 10%,全球 SARS-CoV-2 活动仍然较低。乙型流感病毒在检测到的流感病毒中占主导地位。
规模、影响与风险
南美洲温带地区、南美洲热带地区、非洲南部和东部、东南亚、中美洲和加勒比地区、西非和中非、西亚和东亚以及南亚的一些国家的流感阳性率升高(>10%)。中美洲和加勒比地区国家以及热带南美洲、西亚和东南亚的单个国家的 SARS-CoV-2 阳性率升高(>10%)。在中美洲和加勒比地区、南美洲热带和温带地区的一些国家以及东非和南亚的单个国家中,RSV 阳性率升高(>10%)。
应对、状态与不确定性
各个国家、地区和领地报告了严重程度评估。据报道,南半球温带和亚热带地区、北半球温带和亚热带地区以及热带地区的流感特异性传播率低于季节性阈值或较低。据报道,综合症传播率也较低或低于季节性阈值。证据中没有详细说明具体的公共卫生应对措施或干预措施。
A high priorityPublic-health news

Global Respiratory Virus Activity: Weekly Update N° 587 - ReliefWeb

News Brief
The WHO's weekly update for week 28 2026 indicates low global respiratory virus activity, with influenza positivity below 10% and SARS-CoV-2 activity remaining low. Influenza B viruses were predominant among influenza detections. Elevated positivity (>10%) was observed in specific regions: influenza in parts of South America, Africa, Asia, Central America, and the Caribbean; SARS-CoV-2 in Central America, the Caribbean, and parts of South America and Asia; and RSV in Central America, the Caribbean, South America, Eastern Africa, and Southern Asia. Transmissibility assessments were generally low or below seasonal thresholds. The report provides a global overview without detailing specific public health responses.
Original source text (English)
Skip to main content Help Log in ReliefWeb Search Content Search What are you looking for? Search|t Updates Countries Disasters Organizations Topics Jobs Training Informing humanitarians worldwide 24/7 — a service provided by UN OCHA World Global Respiratory Virus Activity: Weekly Update N° 587 Format Situation Report Source WHO Posted 23 Jul 2026 Originally published 23 Jul 2026 Origin View original Attachments Download Report (PDF | 5.8 MB) Overview In week 28 2026, influenza positivity remained below 10% and SARS-CoV-2 activity remained low globally. Influenza positivity was around 10% in the tropical areas and southern hemisphere temperate and subtropical areas. RSV positivity also remained low globally. Influenza Globally, influenza detections remained low in week 28 and influenza B viruses were predominant among influenza detections. In the southern hemisphere, influenza percent positivity was elevated (>10%) in some countries in Temperate South America and in single countries in Tropical South America, Southern and Eastern Africa and South-East Asia. Small increases in activity were observed in single countries in Tropical South America and South-East Asia. In the northern hemisphere, influenza percent positivity was elevated (>10%) in some countries in Central America and the Caribbean, Southern and South-East Asia and in single countries in Western and Middle Africa, Western and Eastern Asia. Percent positivity was over 30% in a single country in Southern Asia. Small increases in activity were observed in two countries in Central America and the Caribbean and in a single country in Western Africa. In the zones with elevated positivity, influenza A(H3N2) was predominant in Central America and the Caribbean and Middle Africa; influenza A(H1N1)pdm09 was predominant in Southern Asia; and influenza B was predominant in Tropical South America and Western Africa. Influenza A(H1N1)pdm09 and influenza A(H3N2) were codominant in Western and South-East Asia; and influenza A and B were codominant in Temperate South America, Southern Africa and Eastern Asia. SARS-CoV-2 Globally, SARS-CoV-2 positivity remained stable and low across most reporting countries, with elevated positivity (>10%) reported in countries in Central America and the Caribbean and single countries in Tropical South America, Western and South-East Asia. Small increases in activity were observed in two countries in Central America and the Caribbean and single countries in Tropical South America, Western and South-East Asia. Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) RSV positivity was elevated (>10%) in a few countries in Central America and the Caribbean and Tropical and Temperate South America. Percent positivity was over 30% in single countries in Eastern Africa and Southern Asia. A small increase in activity was observed in a single country in Central America. RSV and influenza activity were both elevated in a few countries in Central America and the Caribbean and Temperate South America and in a single country in Southern Asia. Severity assessment The severity assessments here are reported from countries, areas and territories. Assessments for transmissibility can be reported based on syndromic parameters and/or influenza-specific parameters. In the southern hemisphere temperate and subtropical areas, influenza-specific transmissibility was reported as below seasonal threshold (1) and low (1); transmissibility using syndromic data was low (1). In the northern hemisphere temperate and subtropical areas, influenza-specific transmissibility was reported as below seasonal threshold (10) and low (1); transmissibility using syndromic data was reported as below seasonal threshold (6). Influenza-specific transmissibility was reported as low in one country in the tropical areas. View five news elements
Time
Published on 23 Jul 2026, covering week 28 2026.
Location and population
Global, with specific activity reported in regions including southern hemisphere temperate and subtropical areas, northern hemisphere temperate and subtropical areas, tropical areas, Central America and the Caribbean, South America, Africa, and Asia.
Event
WHO reported global respiratory virus activity for week 28 2026, with influenza positivity below 10% and SARS-CoV-2 activity remaining low globally. Influenza B viruses were predominant among influenza detections.
Scale, impact and risk
Influenza positivity was elevated (>10%) in some countries in Temperate South America, Tropical South America, Southern and Eastern Africa, South-East Asia, Central America and the Caribbean, Western and Middle Africa, Western and Eastern Asia, and Southern Asia. SARS-CoV-2 positivity was elevated (>10%) in countries in Central America and the Caribbean and single countries in Tropical South America, Western and South-East Asia. RSV positivity was elevated (>10%) in a few countries in Central America and the Caribbean, Tropical and Temperate South America, and single countries in Eastern Africa and Southern Asia.
Response, status and uncertainty
Severity assessments were reported from countries, areas, and territories. Influenza-specific transmissibility was reported as below seasonal threshold or low in southern hemisphere temperate and subtropical areas, northern hemisphere temperate and subtropical areas, and tropical areas. Syndromic transmissibility was also reported as low or below seasonal threshold. No specific public health responses or interventions were detailed in the evidence.

A 高优先级公共卫生新闻

世界卫生组织 关于 2027 年南半球流感季节使用的流感病毒 疫苗 成分的磋商 - 世界卫生组织 (WHO)

WHO Consultation on the composition of influenza virus vaccines for use in the 2027 southern hemisphere influenza season - World Health Organization (WHO)
新闻简报世界卫生组织(WHO)将于2026年9月21日至24日在法国里昂世界卫生组织学院举行磋商,以确定2027年南半球季节流感疫苗的成分。这一年度活动汇集了全球专家,分析来自世界卫生组织全球流感 监测 和响应系统 (GISRS) 的 监测 数据,审查流行病毒的抗原和遗传特征,并为 疫苗 更新提出建议。磋商还通过审查人畜共患流感病毒来解决大流行的防范问题。这些建议指导国家监管机构和制造商针对 季节性流感 开发有效的 疫苗。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发表日期:2026-07-24。事件日期:2026年9月21日至24日。
地点与对象
世界卫生组织学院,法国里昂。与会者包括来自世卫组织合作中心、国家流感中心、监管实验室和主题专家的代表。
事件
世界卫生组织关于 2027 年南半球流感季节流感病毒 疫苗 组成的磋商。该事件已确认,涉及分析 监测 数据以更新 疫苗 建议。
规模、影响与风险
没有病例、死亡或暴露的报告。重点是全球流感病毒 监测 和 疫苗 成分更新,以确保针对不断演变的病毒的有效性。
应对、状态与不确定性
此次咨询是世卫组织流感 疫苗 更新常规程序的一部分。这些建议将被国家机构和制药公司采用。所提供的证据中没有报告未解决的信息或限制。
A high priorityPublic-health news

WHO Consultation on the composition of influenza virus vaccines for use in the 2027 southern hemisphere influenza season - World Health Organization (WHO)

News Brief
The World Health Organization (WHO) will hold a consultation from 21 to 24 September 2026 at the WHO Academy in Lyon, France, to determine the composition of influenza vaccines for the 2027 southern hemisphere season. This annual event brings together global experts to analyze surveillance data from the WHO Global Influenza Surveillance and Response System (GISRS), review antigenic and genetic characteristics of circulating viruses, and make recommendations for vaccine updates. The consultation also addresses pandemic preparedness by reviewing zoonotic influenza viruses. These recommendations guide national regulatory agencies and manufacturers in developing effective vaccines against seasonal influenza.
Original source text (English)

Credits WHO Consultation on the composition of influenza virus vaccines for use in the 2027 southern hemisphere influenza season 21 – 24 September 2026 WHO Academy, Lyon, France Reading time: 1 min (337 words) WHO ConsultationBackgroundInfluenza vaccines must be updated regularly to remain effective against constantly evolving circulating viruses.Twice each year, the World Health Organization (WHO) organizes consultations with an advisory group of experts to analyse influenza virus surveillance data generated by the WHO Global Influenza Surveillance and Response System (GISRS) and issues recommendations on the composition of the influenza vaccines for the following influenza season. These recommendations are used by national vaccine regulatory agencies and pharmaceutical companies to develop, produce and license influenza vaccines.ObjectivesAnalyse the antigenic and genetic characteristics of seasonal influenza viruses circulating and infecting humans, taking into consideration the available epidemiological and clinical information from individual countries and regions;Make recommendations on the composition of influenza virus vaccines for use in the 2027 southern hemisphere influenza season; andReview the antigenic and genetic characteristics of recent A(H5) and other subtypes or variant zoonotic influenza viruses, that the WHO Collaborating Centres of the GISRS received and the need to develop new candidate vaccine viruses for pandemic preparedness purposes.Participants (by invitation)Representatives from WHO Collaborating Centres for influenza of GISRS;Representatives from WHO Essential Regulatory Laboratories of GISRS;Representatives from National Influenza Centres and WHO H5 Reference Laboratories of GISRS;Representatives from the WHO Collaborating Centre for Modelling, Evolution, and Control of Emerging Infectious Diseases; Representatives from the Food and Agriculture Organization of the United Nations/World Organisation for Animal Health Network of Expertise on Animal Influenza (OFFLU); andOther subject matter expertsProvisional main agenda itemsReview of antigenic and genetic characterization data of seasonal influenza viruses, A(H5) and other subtypes or variant influenza viruses analysed by the WHO Collaborating Centres and other reference laboratories;Review of global influenza activity;Analysis of results of vaccine serological studies;Recommendations for the composition of influenza virus vaccines for use in the 2027 southern hemisphere influenza season; andReview of the need and subsequent selection of new A(H5) and other subtypes or variant viruses for the development of new candidate vaccine viruses for pandemic preparedness purposes. Events WHO Information Meeting on the composition of influenza virus vaccines for use in the 2027 southern hemisphere influenza season 25 September 2026 09:00 – 12:00 UTC Time
View five news elements
Time
Publication date: 2026-07-24. Event date: 21 – 24 September 2026.
Location and population
WHO Academy, Lyon, France. Participants include representatives from WHO Collaborating Centres, National Influenza Centres, regulatory laboratories, and subject matter experts.
Event
WHO Consultation on the composition of influenza virus vaccines for the 2027 southern hemisphere influenza season. The event is confirmed and involves analyzing surveillance data to update vaccine recommendations.
Scale, impact and risk
No cases, deaths, or exposures reported. The focus is on global influenza virus surveillance and vaccine composition updates to ensure effectiveness against evolving viruses.
Response, status and uncertainty
The consultation is part of routine WHO procedures for influenza vaccine updates. Recommendations will be used by national agencies and pharmaceutical companies. No unresolved information or limitations reported in the supplied evidence.

更新 - 塔斯马尼亚的急性呼吸道感染活动 - 塔斯马尼亚卫生部

Update - acute respiratory infection activity in Tasmania - Tasmanian Department of Health
新闻简报塔斯马尼亚卫生部发布了截至 2026 年 7 月 19 日急性呼吸道感染活动的最新情况。虽然急性呼吸道疾病 (ARI) 仍然较低,但正在逐渐增加。与近年来相比,COVID-19 活动较低,流感活动也较低且低于预期水平。呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 活性中等,并按照季节性模式持续上升。在流感和 RSV 高峰季节,该部门每两周更新一次此信息,并指导公众查看 RespTas 报告以获取详细的 监测 数据。鼓励居民随时了解情况并采取预防措施,以减少 传播 风险。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发表于2026-07-24;截至 2026 年 7 月 19 日星期日的活动数据。
地点与对象
澳大利亚塔斯马尼亚;受急性呼吸道感染影响的普通人群。
事件
塔斯马尼亚卫生部报告了急性呼吸道感染活动的官方更新,包括 COVID-19、流感和 RSV。
规模、影响与风险
急性呼吸道疾病活动仍然较低,但逐渐增加;COVID-19 活动低;流感活动低且低于预期水平;RSV 活动温和、不断增加,并且与季节模式一致。
应对、状态与不确定性
在流感和 RSV 活动增加期间每两周提供一次更新;RespTas 报告中提供了详细的 监测;建议公众遵循活动水平指南以降低风险。
A high priorityPublic-health news

Update - acute respiratory infection activity in Tasmania - Tasmanian Department of Health

News Brief
The Tasmanian Department of Health has issued an update on acute respiratory infection activity as of 19 July 2026. While acute respiratory illness (ARI) remains low, it is gradually increasing. COVID-19 activity is low, and influenza activity is low and below expected levels compared to recent years. Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) activity is moderate, continuing to rise in line with seasonal patterns. The department updates this information fortnightly during heightened influenza and RSV seasons and directs the public to the RespTas report for detailed surveillance data. Residents are encouraged to stay informed and take preventive measures to reduce transmission risks.
Original source text (English)
